# Plugins · S · part 15

1,304 entries, strategy-coach to super-team. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **strategy-coach**: Document what the business is and specify what comes next: an Obsidian vault of actors, processes and rules mapped to the code that implements them, and feature specs with a checkable definition of done — /blueprint, /feature. Inward-facing; competitors and industry rules moved to atlas-coach. ([MohammedMoataz/ai-coach](https://github.com/MohammedMoataz/ai-coach/tree/HEAD/plugins/strategy-coach) · 2 skills)
- **strategy-consultant**: Runs a full strategy consulting engagement end-to-end — from sharpening a vague business question into a decision-oriented problem statement, through parallel multi-agent research with independent validation, sense-checking for bias and analytical drift, storyline synthesis, to a sourced, executive-grade client report (.docx) ready for expert review. Designed to produce 80–90% of the insight as a high-quality working deliverable, so consultants can focus on the 10–20% that creates unique value: client judgment, proprietary insight, and sharpening the final recommendations. ([Chipkorvyn/strategy-consultant](https://github.com/Chipkorvyn/strategy-consultant))

Extensible to robustness, walk-forward, and regime evaluators. ([martianmobile/strategy-evaluation](https://github.com/martianmobile/strategy-evaluation/tree/HEAD/plugins/strategy-evaluation) · 1 skill)

- **strategy-frameworks**: Strategic Thinking Agent — meta-router for 47 business strategy frameworks (Porter Five Forces, Drucker MBO/5Q, Balanced Scorecard, Blue Ocean, Wardley Mapping, BCG Matrix, BMC, Lean Startup, OKR, Disruptive Innovation, Scenario Planning, Real Options, Game Theory, TRIZ, TOC, and more). Built on Korean business context (SME ecosystem, chaebol dynamics, regulatory environment). Invoke /think to auto-classify problems and select 1–3 optimal frameworks. - **strategy-grid**: Judge candidate options against named conditions as an independent M×N grid, scored in your own blind subagents. Every pairing gets three relations — push, grip, stake — then the grid is read by rows to rank the options and by columns to score the set as a strategy. Works on a repo refactor question or a business plan; no server and no API key. Ships with strategy-comms, which writes a grid result up for whoever decides and reworks any draft so a senior reader can act on it. ([commahawk/strategy-grid](https://github.com/commahawk/strategy-grid) · 2 skills, 2 agents)

Logs every pick, the model it replaced and the estimated cost difference to ~/.claude/mods-logs/savings.jsonl. No API key and no network call of its own.
